## Corpus Check
- 7053 files · ~8,795,979 words

## Summary
- 54424 nodes · 111189 edges · 4103 communities (2378 shown, 1725 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7332 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `031d5e59`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
